# 78. API naming

Brand:

```text
NurResult
NurError
NurOption
NurStatus
```

Actual C identifiers:

```c
nur_result_int
nur_error_t
nur_option_int
nur_status_t
```

Macro namespace:

```c
NUR_OK
NUR_ERR
NUR_TRY_ASSIGN
```

এতে C namespace pollution কমবে।

---

# 79. `NUR_DEFINE_RESULT`

আপনার original:

```c
#define DEFINE_NUR_RESULT(type, name)
```

এর বদলে:

```c
#define NUR_DEFINE_RESULT(Type, Name)
```

আমি recommend করব।

একটি consistent namespace।

---

# 80. Reference basic implementation

একটি conceptual C11 foundation:

```c
#ifndef NUR_RESULT_H
#define NUR_RESULT_H

#include <stdbool.h>

typedef enum {
    NUR_E_NONE = 0,
    NUR_E_INVALID_ARGUMENT,
    NUR_E_OUT_OF_RANGE,
    NUR_E_NOT_FOUND,
    NUR_E_PERMISSION,
    NUR_E_IO,
    NUR_E_PARSE,
    NUR_E_TIMEOUT,
    NUR_E_CANCELLED,
    NUR_E_OUT_OF_MEMORY,
    NUR_E_UNSUPPORTED,
    NUR_E_INTERNAL
} nur_error_code_t;

typedef struct {
    nur_error_code_t code;
    int system_code;

    const char *message;
    const char *context;

    const char *file;
    const char *function;
    unsigned line;

} nur_error_t;

#define NUR_DEFINE_RESULT(Type, Name)              \
    typedef struct {                               \
        bool is_ok;                                \
        union {                                    \
            Type value;                            \
            nur_error_t error;                    \
        } data;                                    \
    } Name

#define NUR_OK(Name, value)                        \
    ((Name){                                       \
        .is_ok = true,                             \
        .data.value = (value)                      \
    })

#define NUR_ERR(Name, error_value)                \
    ((Name){                                       \
        .is_ok = false,                            \
        .data.error = (error_value)                \
    })

#define NUR_IS_OK(r)   ((r).is_ok)
#define NUR_IS_ERR(r)  (!(r).is_ok)

#define NUR_VALUE(r)   ((r).data.value)
#define NUR_ERROR(r)   ((r).data.error)

#define NUR_IGNORE(x) ((void)(x))

#endif
```

এটি production-ready library নয়; এটি clean baseline architecture।

---

# 81. Error constructor

Concept:

```c
#define NUR_MAKE_ERROR(code_, message_) \
    ((nur_error_t){                     \
        .code = (code_),                \
        .system_code = 0,               \
        .message = (message_),          \
        .context = NULL,                \
        .file = __FILE__,               \
        .function = __func__,           \
        .line = __LINE__                \
    })
```

এখানে macro parameter names member names-এর মতো না রাখাই ভালো।

---

# 82. Use:

```c
NUR_DEFINE_RESULT(int, nur_result_int);

nur_result_int divide(int a, int b)
{
    if (b == 0) {
        return NUR_ERR(
            nur_result_int,
            NUR_MAKE_ERROR(
                NUR_E_INVALID_ARGUMENT,
                "division by zero"
            )
        );
    }

    return NUR_OK(nur_result_int, a / b);
}
```

Caller:

```c
nur_result_int r = divide(10, 2);

if (NUR_IS_OK(r)) {
    printf("%d\n", NUR_VALUE(r));
} else {
    printf(
        "error: %s\n",
        NUR_ERROR(r).message
    );
}
```

---

# 83. কেন এটি আপনার original code-এর তুলনায় অনেক ভালো?

Original:

```text
error = string
```

Improved:

```text
error
 ├── code
 ├── system_code
 ├── message
 ├── context
 └── source
```

এতে machine logic এবং human diagnostics আলাদা হয়।

---

# 84. `NUR_TRY_ASSIGN` baseline

```c
#define NUR_TRY_ASSIGN(ResultType, out, expression)  \
    do {                                              \
        ResultType nur_tmp = (expression);             \
        if (!nur_tmp.is_ok)                           \
            return nur_tmp;                           \
        (out) = nur_tmp.data.value;                   \
    } while (0)
```

Usage:

```c
nur_result_int process(void)
{
    int a;
    NUR_TRY_ASSIGN(nur_result_int, a, foo());

    int b;
    NUR_TRY_ASSIGN(nur_result_int, b, bar(a));

    return NUR_OK(nur_result_int, b);
}
```

---

# 85. Macro safety-এর নিয়ম

`nur.h` macro library হলে এগুলো mandatory:

```text
single evaluation
parenthesized arguments
do { } while (0)
no generic macro collisions
private NUR_DETAIL_ names
```

বিশেষ করে macro argument দুইবার evaluate করা যাবে না।

---

# 86. Example of a dangerous macro

খারাপ:

```c
#define BAD(x) ((x) + (x))
```

এখানে:

```c
BAD(counter++)
```

dangerous।

Nur macros-এ এমন pattern avoid করতে হবে।

---

# 87. Error source location-এর advantage

Error:

```text
NUR_E_PARSE
```

এর চেয়ে:

```text
NUR_E_PARSE
config.c:84
parse_port()
context="server.port"
```

debugging-এর জন্য অনেক বেশি valuable।

---

# 88. But source location should not be part of semantic identity

এগুলো:

```text
file
line
function
```

debug metadata।

Application logic should never do:

```c
if (error.line == 84)
```

Semantic logic:

```c
if (error.code == NUR_E_PARSE)
```

---

# 89. Error message should not be semantic identity

Never:

```c
if (strcmp(err.message, "timeout") == 0)
```

Always:

```c
if (err.code == NUR_E_TIMEOUT)
```

---

# 90. Error formatting layer

Possible:

```c
int nur_error_format(
    const nur_error_t *err,
    char *buffer,
    size_t capacity
);
```

এতে caller owns buffer।

এটি:

```text
allocation-free
embedded-friendly
predictable
```

---

# 91. `nur_error_print` optional

```c
void nur_error_print(const nur_error_t *);
```

এটি core header-এ না রেখে:

```text
nur_diagnostics.h
```

এ রাখা যেতে পারে।

কারণ সব project `stdio` চায় না।

---

# 92. No hidden logging

Nur function-এর ভিতরে secretly:

```c
fprintf(stderr, ...)
```

করবেন না।

Library শুধু return করবে:

```text
what happened
```

Application সিদ্ধান্ত নেবে:

```text
log?
retry?
fallback?
user message?
abort?
```

---

# 93. Retry semantics

`NUR_E_TIMEOUT` automatically retry করা উচিত?

না।

কারণ:

```text
GET request
```

এ retry হয়তো safe।

কিন্তু:

```text
POST payment
```

এ blind retry dangerous হতে পারে।

তাই:

> error classification ≠ retry policy

---

# 94. Cancelled আলাদা error

Async/long-running operation-এর জন্য:

```c
NUR_E_CANCELLED
```

খুব useful।

এটি ordinary I/O failure-এর সঙ্গে মিশিয়ে দেবেন না।

---

# 95. Timeout আলাদা

```c
NUR_E_TIMEOUT
```

কারণ application:

```text
retry
fallback
offline mode
```

করতে পারে।

---

# 96. Internal errors

```c
NUR_E_INTERNAL
```

generic fallback হিসেবে থাকতে পারে।

কিন্তু developers-কে discourage করুন:

```c
return NUR_ERR(..., NUR_E_INTERNAL);
```

সবকিছুর জন্য ব্যবহার করতে।

---

# 97. Error codes too many হলে কী হবে?

যদি:

```text
300 error codes
```

হয়, developer memorize করতে পারবে না।

তাই core codes কম রাখুন।

Domain-specific libraries নিজেদের code namespace maintain করবে।

---

# 98. X-macro approach

Error enum এবং string table synchronize করার জন্য X-macro excellent।

Concept:

```c
#define NUR_ERROR_LIST(X) \
    X(NUR_E_NONE) \
    X(NUR_E_INVALID_ARGUMENT) \
    X(NUR_E_NOT_FOUND) \
    X(NUR_E_PERMISSION) \
    X(NUR_E_IO) \
    X(NUR_E_TIMEOUT)
```

তারপর এই এক তালিকা থেকে:

```text
enum
name strings
documentation
test cases
```

generate করতে পারেন।

---

# 99. এটি Nur-এর জন্য একটি ভালো metaprogramming trick

কারণ C-এর enum-to-string boilerplate manually maintain করতে হয়।

X-macro single source of truth তৈরি করে।

---

# 100. `NUR_ERROR_CODE_NAME`

X-macro থেকে:

```c
const char *nur_error_code_name(
    nur_error_code_t code
);
```

generate করা সহজ।

---

# 101. Error code numeric stability

যদি codes logs/protocols-এ persist হয়, explicit numbering consider করুন:

```c
NUR_E_NONE = 0,
NUR_E_INVALID_ARGUMENT = 1,
NUR_E_NOT_FOUND = 2,
...
```

কিন্তু পরে নতুন values মাঝখানে ঢুকিয়ে existing numeric mapping বদলাবেন না।

---

# 102. Result + C ABI

Public binary library হলে Result struct-এর layout ABI concern হয়ে দাঁড়ায়।

Header-only হলে source-level integration সহজ।

যদি compiled shared library বানান:

```text
ABI version
struct layout
alignment
calling convention
version compatibility
```

consider করতে হবে।

---

# 103. Header-only কেন attractive

NurResult-এর implementation mostly:

```text
macros
structs
static inline
```

হতে পারে।

তাই header-only:

```text
easy install
easy use
no linking headache
```

হতে পারে।

---

# 104. Existing result libraries থেকে শেখার বিষয়

একটি historical C implementation `result.h` Rust-inspired Result model ব্যবহার করেছে। ([Gist][1])

আর C++ result ecosystem-এ rich features যেমন:

```text
map
flat_map
map_error
void
reference/value
testing
```

দেখা যায়। ([GitHub][17])

এ থেকে একটি lesson:

> Result abstraction mature হলে developers শুধু `Ok/Err` চায় না; composition এবং ergonomics চায়।

---

# 105. কিন্তু C-তে C++ feature copy করবেন না

C++ implementation থেকে:

```text
templates
operator overloading
constructors
destructors
lambdas
```

copy করা সম্ভব নয়।

Nur-এর strength হবে:

```text
C-native idioms
```

---

# 106. `map`, `and_then`, `or_else`

Future functional API:

```text
map
map_error
and_then
or_else
inspect
```

এগুলো Rust/C++ result ecosystems-এর পরিচিত composition patterns। C++ `std::expected`-এও modern monadic operations যেমন `and_then`, `or_else`, `transform`, `transform_error` আছে। ([C++ Reference][18])

কিন্তু Nur-এ প্রথম release-এ এগুলো না রাখাই ভালো।

---

# 107. কেন?

কারণ C-তে:

```text
function pointer
generic macros
type inference
function return types
```

নিয়ে composition API দ্রুত complex হয়ে যায়।

---

# 108. `NUR_AND_THEN` v2

Concept:

```c
result_a
  → if OK
    → function(result_a.value)
  → if ERR
    → propagate
```

এতে nested error boilerplate কমে।

কিন্তু first milestone নয়।

---

# 109. `NUR_MAP` v3

Success value transform:

```text
Result<int>
    ↓ map(square)
Result<int>
```

এটি functional programming enthusiasts-এর জন্য useful।

---

# 110. `NUR_MAP_ERROR`

Error transform:

```text
Result<T, FileError>
      ↓
Result<T, ConfigError>
```

এটি বড় application architecture-এ useful।

---

# 111. Error wrapping বনাম error mapping

### Mapping

```text
one error type → another error type
```

### Wrapping

```text
high-level context
+
original cause
```

দুটো আলাদা feature।

---

# 112. Result chain

Ideal high-level API:

```text
read_file()
    ↓
parse_config()
    ↓
validate_config()
    ↓
connect()
```

যদি প্রতিটি Result দেয়, full operation:

```text
Result<Config>
```

এবং failure automatically propagate হতে পারে।

---

# 113. This is the real “coding style change”

পুরোনো C:

```text
return code
+
check
+
errno
+
cleanup
```

Nur:

```text
Result
+
TRY
+
structured error
```

এটি সত্যিই style-level shift।

---

# 114. But cleanup remains a major problem

ধরুন:

```c
file = open();
buffer = malloc();
config = parse();
```

`parse()` fail করলে:

```text
free(buffer)
close(file)
return error
```

করতে হবে।

Result শুধু propagation solve করে।

---

# 115. Therefore `NUR_DEFER` is a possible future feature

Concept:

```c
NUR_DEFER(nur_close(file));
NUR_DEFER(free(buffer));
```

Scope exit-এ cleanup।

কিন্তু C macro-based defer implementation tricky হতে পারে।

---

# 116. Defer should remain a separate module

```text
nur_result
```

এ `defer` embed করবেন না।

Architecture:

```text
nur.h
 ├─ result
 ├─ error
 ├─ option
 ├─ status
 └─ optional defer
```

---

# 117. Why Defer is not your flagship

কারণ:

```text
GNU extensions
scope tricks
macro complexity
control-flow corner cases
```

এর কারণে portability খারাপ হতে পারে।

Result + nodiscard + try অনেক cleaner।

---

# 118. Result + ownership future vision

Long term আপনি resource wrappers বানাতে পারেন:

```text
NurFile
NurBuffer
NurSocket
NurLock
NurHandle
```

প্রতিটি explicit cleanup API সহ।

---

# 119. Example

```c
nur_result_file file = nur_open(path);

if (NUR_IS_ERR(file))
    return NUR_ERROR(file);
```

এখানে `nur_result_file` success হলে:

```text
handle
```

error হলে:

```text
nur_error_t
```

---

# 120. Large values-এর জন্য out-param API

Result by value সব সময় ideal নয়।

Huge object:

```c
nur_result_huge_object
```

potentially large।

Alternative:

```c
nur_status_t nur_parse(
    input,
    huge_object *out
);
```

এখানে output storage caller owns।

---

# 121. Best design rule

### Small/simple result

```text
return Result<T>
```

### Large object

```text
Status + out parameter
```

এতে performance predictable থাকে।

---

# 122. “Zero-cost” কীভাবে সত্যিকারভাবে evaluate করবেন

Benchmark করুন:

```text
baseline:
int + sentinel

baseline:
int + errno

candidate:
Result<int>

candidate:
Result<int> + structured error
```

Measure:

```text
binary size
instruction count
branch count
stack usage
success latency
failure latency
```

---

# 123. Compiler optimization levels

Test:

```text
-O0
-O1
-O2
-O3
-Os
```

কারণ Result-এর cost optimization-dependent হতে পারে।

---

# 124. Hardware diversity

কমপক্ষে:

```text
x86-64
ARM64
```

কারণ aggregate return conventions আলাদা হতে পারে।

---

# 125. Compiler matrix

At minimum:

```text
GCC
Clang
MSVC
```

and:

```text
C11
C17
C23
```

C23 feature support compiler অনুযায়ী এখনও uniform নয়; তাই C23-specific features optional compile mode-এ রাখা উচিত। ([C++ Reference][11])

---

# 126. `NUR_NODISCARD` capability detection

Ideal design:

```text
C23 native
↓
GCC/Clang extension
↓
MSVC-compatible route where practical
↓
empty fallback
```

Unsupported compiler-এ library break না করে safety feature gracefully degrade করবে।

---

# 127. Security model

NurResult itself security library নয়।

কিন্তু structured errors security-এর জন্য useful হতে পারে।

কারণ:

```text
internal diagnostics
```

এবং:

```text
external message
```

আলাদা করা যায়।

---

# 128. Example security mistake

Internal:

```text
permission denied opening:
C:\Users\Admin\AppData\...
```

User-facing:

```text
Unable to open configuration file.
```

`NurError` internal structure রেখে UI sanitized message দেখাতে পারে।

---

# 129. Error classification for security

Possible:

```text
NUR_E_PERMISSION
NUR_E_AUTH
NUR_E_ACCESS
```

কিন্তু security subsystem-specific errors application layer-এ রাখা better।

---

# 130. Secret leakage

Never casually put:

```text
password
API key
token
private key
```

error context-এ।

একটি error framework-এর documentation-এ explicit warning থাকা উচিত।

---

# 131. Thread safety

Core Result ideally value-oriented।

এতে:

```text
thread A
    owns Result A

thread B
    owns Result B
```

global registry দরকার হয় না।

এটি explicit state architecture-এর natural benefit।

---

# 132. Do not create global “last Nur error”

এটি খুব important।

এভাবে:

```c
nur_last_error()
```

global/thread-local registry করলে Result-এর biggest conceptual advantage নষ্ট হতে পারে।

তখন আবার:

```text
hidden ambient state
```

ফিরে আসে।

---

# 133. Result-এ error travel করবে

পুরোনো:

```text
value → caller
error → side channel
```

Nur:

```text
+-------------------+
| Result            |
|                   |
| OK -> value       |
| ERR -> error      |
+-------------------+
```

একটি object-এ সব।

---

# 134. Explicitness is the actual selling point

Nur বলছে না:

> C crashes.

বরং:

> **Failure is part of the function contract, so make it visible in the type.**

উদাহরণ:

```c
nur_result_config load_config(...);
```

এই signature দেখেই বোঝা যায়:

```text
success/failure দুইটাই expected।
```

---

# 135. Function contract becomes inspectable

Documentation:

```text
Returns:
    configuration

Errors:
    NUR_E_NOT_FOUND
    NUR_E_PERMISSION
    NUR_E_PARSE
```

একটি mature API-তে এটি enormous advantage।

---

# 136. Doxygen integration

আপনি Doxygen-style contract conventions তৈরি করতে পারেন:

```c
/**
 * @return Result containing parsed configuration.
 *
 * @error NUR_E_NOT_FOUND
 * @error NUR_E_PERMISSION
 * @error NUR_E_PARSE
 */
```

Future docs generator এই data পড়তে পারে।

---

# 137. Static analysis — সম্ভাব্য আসল killer feature

এখানেই আমি মনে করি Nur ভবিষ্যতে genuinely interesting হতে পারে।

Imagine:

```c
nur_result_int r = foo();

int x = r.data.value;
```

একটি Nur-aware analyzer বলে:

```text
warning:
NurResult value accessed without checking success state
```

এটি plain Result macro-এর চেয়ে অনেক বেশি powerful।

---

# 138. কেন?

কারণ C type system:

```text
r.data.value
```

এর সঙ্গে:

```text
r.is_ok
```

এর data-flow relation enforce করে না।

কিন্তু static analyzer করতে পারে:

```text
if (r.is_ok)
    r.data.value;
```

safe path।

---

# 139. সম্ভাব্য Nur analyzer

Future command:

```bash
nur-check main.c
```

Find:

```text
unchecked_result
unchecked_union_access
ignored_result
invalid_error_use
dangling_error_message
```

---

# 140. IDE integration

VS Code/clangd extension:

```text
NUR_E_NOT_FOUND
```

hover করলে:

```text
Meaning:
Resource was not found.

Recoverability:
Usually recoverable.

Suggested actions:
fallback / create / propagate
```

এটি খুব futuristic কিন্তু practical direction।

---

# 141. Compiler-assisted source generation

IDE autocomplete:

```text
NUR_...
```

দিলে:

```text
NUR_E_INVALID_ARGUMENT
NUR_E_NOT_FOUND
NUR_E_PERMISSION
NUR_E_TIMEOUT
```

show হতে পারে।

---

# 142. Error catalog

`nur_error_codes.json`:

```json
{
  "NUR_E_NOT_FOUND": {
    "severity": "error",
    "category": "resource",
    "recoverable": true
  }
}
```

এই metadata থেকে:

```text
docs
IDE
tests
string tables
```

generate করা যায়।

এটি core library নয়; ecosystem tool।

---

# 143. Error domain metadata

Future:

```text
domain = FILE
code = NOT_FOUND
```

এতে একই semantic namespace বিভিন্ন subsystem-এ পরিষ্কার হতে পারে।

---

# 144. Error conversion registry

ধারণাগত:

```text
POSIX ENOENT
      ↓
NUR_E_NOT_FOUND

Win32 ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND
      ↓
NUR_E_NOT_FOUND
```

একই application logic OS-neutral হয়ে যায়।

---

# 145. This is much stronger than simply “Rust Result in C”

কারণ:

```text
Rust comparison:
Result abstraction
```

কিন্তু Nur proposition:

```text
cross-platform explicit error vocabulary
```

এটি C ecosystem-এর জন্য বেশি relevant।

---

# 146. `NurResult` এবং operating system boundaries

Core:

```text
Nur semantic world
```

Adapters:

```text
POSIX world
Windows world
embedded world
network world
```

এটি layered design।

---

# 147. Freestanding/embedded mode

C-এর বড় ব্যবহার embedded systems।

সুতরাং:

```text
nur_core
```

এ ideally:

```text
no malloc
no stdio
no OS
no threads
```

থাকতে পারে।

এটি library adoption dramatically বাড়াতে পারে।

---

# 148. Embedded-friendly error

```c
typedef struct {
    nur_error_code_t code;
    int system_code;
} nur_error_minimal_t;
```

Diagnostic strings optional।

---

# 149. Real-time considerations

Real-time code-এ unpredictable heap allocation/logging undesirable হতে পারে।

Nur core যদি:

```text
allocation-free
no hidden locks
no logging
```

হয়, তা real-time friendly হতে পারে।

তবে “hard real-time safe” claim benchmark এবং platform analysis ছাড়া করবেন না।

---

# 150. No exceptions

C-তে standard exception mechanism নেই।

Nur explicit return পথ ব্যবহার করে:

```text
no hidden stack unwinding
no runtime exception framework
```

এটি embedded এবং predictable systems-এ attractive।

---

# 151. `setjmp/longjmp` ব্যবহার করবেন?

NurResult core-এ না।

কারণ এটি explicit Result philosophy থেকে অনেক দূরে যায় এবং resource/control-flow handling জটিল করে।

---

# 152. Error handling strategy table

| Approach           | Explicit |   Typed | Context | Easy propagation | Core C |
| ------------------ | -------: | ------: | ------: | ---------------: | -----: |
| `-1`               |        ❌ |       ❌ |       ❌ |               ⚠️ |      ✅ |
| `NULL`             |        ❌ |       ❌ |       ❌ |               ⚠️ |      ✅ |
| `errno`            |       ⚠️ | partial |      ⚠️ |                ❌ |      ✅ |
| status code        |        ✅ |   ✅-ish |      ⚠️ |               ⚠️ |      ✅ |
| out-param + status |        ✅ |   ✅-ish |      ⚠️ |               ⚠️ |      ✅ |
| Result struct      |        ✅ |       ✅ |       ✅ |                ✅ |      ✅ |
| Rust `Result`      |        ✅ |       ✅ |       ✅ |               ✅✅ |      ❌ |
| Go `(T,error)`     |        ✅ |       ✅ |       ✅ |                ✅ |      ❌ |

Nur aims to capture the bottom-center benefits while remaining idiomatic C.

---

# 153. C++-এর `std::expected` থেকে কী শেখা যায়?

C++23-এ `std::expected<T,E>` standard vocabulary type হিসেবে আছে এবং expected value বা unexpected error-এর one-of-two model দেয়; এর modern API-তে monadic operations-ও আছে। ([C++ Reference][19])

Nur-এর lesson:

```text
Result needs:
    observation
    transformation
    propagation
```

শুধু storage না।

---

# 154. কিন্তু C++ `expected`-কে clone করবেন না

C++ templates, constructors, destructors, concepts etc. C-তে নেই।

Nur should be:

```text
C-native expected/result
```

---

# 155. Result + Option + Status — তিনটি vocabulary type যথেষ্ট

আমি শুরুতে এর বাইরে যেতাম না:

```text
Result<T>
Option<T>
Status
```

কারণ এ তিনটি একসঙ্গে:

```text
success value
absence
failure
```

এই তিনটি fundamental states cover করে।

---

# 156. Future state machine

Nur Result itself:

```text
UNINITIALIZED?
```

state যোগ করা উচিত কি?

না।

Result-এর only valid states:

```text
OK
ERR
```

Uninitialized variable থাকলে সেটা API misuse।

---

# 157. `NUR_RESULT_INIT`

Debug helper থাকতে পারে:

```c
NUR_RESULT_ZERO
```

কিন্তু সাধারণভাবে:

```c
nur_result_int r;
```

এর পরে use করা dangerous।

Compiler/toolchain initialization warnings enable করা better।

---

# 158. Invalid Result detection

Debug builds-এ internal assertions:

```text
state must be known
```

রাখা যায়।

কিন্তু production Result object-এ third “invalid” variant যোগ করলে size/logic complexity বাড়ে।

---

# 159. `bool` বনাম enum tag

`bool` works।

কিন্তু:

```c
typedef enum {
    NUR_ERR_STATE,
    NUR_OK_STATE
} nur_result_state_t;
```

semantically clearer হতে পারে।

Potential future:

```text
OK
ERR
```

এবং debugging output পরিষ্কার।

---

# 160. আমি v1-এ enum tag পছন্দ করব

```c
typedef enum {
    NUR_RESULT_ERR = 0,
    NUR_RESULT_OK = 1
} nur_result_state_t;
```

তারপর:

```c
typedef struct {
    nur_result_state_t state;

    union {
        T value;
        nur_error_t error;
    } data;
} ...
```

---

# 161. কিন্তু `bool` কেন still acceptable?

কারণ:

```text
small
obvious
C-native
```

যদি performance/size-sensitive implementation হয়।

এটি style decision, fundamental architectural decision নয়।

---

# 162. `NUR_IS_ERR` লিখবেন

```c
#define NUR_IS_ERR(r) (!(r).is_ok)
```

এটি semantics clearly expresses।

---

# 163. `NUR_VALUE` এবং `NUR_ERROR` raw member access hide করবে

Public API:

```c
NUR_VALUE(r)
NUR_ERROR(r)
```

future implementation change easier।

---

# 164. Safe accessor future

Potential:

```c
bool nur_result_get(
    result,
    T *out
);
```

কিন্তু C-তে type-generated accessor boilerplate বাড়ে।

v1-এ macro enough।

---

# 165. `NUR_UNWRAP` debug behavior

Debug:

```text
print error
abort
```

Release:

```text
abort
```

Optional custom handler।

---

# 166. `NUR_EXPECT`

Perhaps:

```c
config = NUR_EXPECT(
    load_config(),
    "configuration is mandatory"
);
```

Error হলে enriched diagnostic।

কিন্তু v1-এ unnecessary।

---

# 167. `NUR_PANIC`

Potential:

```c
NUR_PANIC("unreachable");
```

source location automatically included।

---

# 168. `NUR_ASSERT`

```c
NUR_ASSERT(ptr != NULL);
```

Result system-এর companion but separate responsibility।

---

# 169. Diagnostics callback

Advanced:

```c
typedef void (*nur_panic_handler_t)(
    const char *message,
    const char *file,
    unsigned line,
    const char *function
);
```

Custom embedded/server behavior সম্ভব।

---

# 170. Error handlers should not be global by default

One global callback may be okay for panic:

```text
panic handler
```

but not for ordinary errors.

Ordinary errors should travel with Result.

---

# 171. `NUR_RESULT_MAP` ভবিষ্যতে possible

Conceptual:

```c
NUR_MAP(result, transform)
```

success হলে transform করে new Result।

But function pointers add indirect calls, and generic typing gets complicated.

---

# 172. Performance of `map`

For trivial transformations:

```text
macro expansion
```

could inline.

Function-pointer implementation:

```text
potential indirect call
```

So API choice matters.

---

# 173. Better v1 style

Use ordinary C:

```c
if (NUR_IS_OK(r)) {
    ...
}
```

No functional programming tax.

---

# 174. Result ergonomics hierarchy

Best to worst:

```text
1. NUR_TRY_ASSIGN
2. NUR_IS_OK
3. NUR_UNWRAP
4. NUR_MAP
5. complex monadic macros
```

v1 focus 1–3।

---

# 175. Testing strategy — absolutely essential

Test categories:

```text
construction
state
value
error
copy
assignment
return
nested propagation
errno
compiler diagnostics
```

---

# 176. Basic tests

```text
OK int
ERR int
OK pointer
ERR pointer
OK struct
ERR struct
```

---

# 177. Error tests

```text
code preserved
message preserved
context preserved
system_code preserved
source location preserved
```

---

# 178. Lifetime tests

Test that documentation prevents:

```text
stack message dangling
temporary context dangling
freed pointer context
```

---

# 179. Macro tests

Test:

```text
expression evaluated once
parentheses correct
nested macro correct
comma expressions
function calls
side effects
```

---

# 180. Compiler diagnostics tests

Test:

```c
NUR_NODISCARD nur_result_int foo(void);
```

then:

```c
foo();
```

Expected warning where supported।

C23 allows nodiscard diagnostics; GCC supports `warn_unused_result` as an extension. ([C++ Reference][11])

---

# 181. Intentional discard test

```c
NUR_IGNORE(foo());
```

should suppress the nodiscard diagnostic where the compiler follows the documented cast-to-void semantics; C23's `nodiscard` specifically exempts casts to `void`. ([C++ Reference][11])

---

# 182. Sanitizers

CI:

```text
ASan
UBSan
LSan
TSan
```

যেখানে applicable।

Result library itself isn't a memory-safety system, তাই sanitizer testing খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ।

---

# 183. Fuzzing

Especially:

```text
parse
deserialize
decode
validate
```

NurResult makes expected invalid input representable as:

```text
ERR
```

instead of crash.

---

# 184. Fuzzing invariant

একটি parser idealistically:

```text
valid input
    → OK

invalid input
    → ERR(PARSE)

memory violation
    → NEVER
```

এই property test করা যায়।

---

# 185. Example application to demonstrate Nur

সবচেয়ে effective demo আমি বানাতাম:

```text
config loader
```

Pipeline:

```text
file open
    ↓
file read
    ↓
parse
    ↓
validate
    ↓
return config
```

প্রতিটি stage:

```text
Result
```

দেবে।

---

# 186. Demo traditional C

```text
open
  ↓
if NULL
  ↓
errno
  ↓
read
  ↓
if < 0
  ↓
errno
  ↓
parse
  ↓
if invalid
  ↓
cleanup
```

---

# 187. Demo Nur

```text
open
 ↓
Result<File>
 ↓ TRY
read
 ↓
Result<Text>
 ↓ TRY
parse
 ↓
Result<Config>
```

এটি developer immediately understand করবে।

---

# 188. Killer README example

আমি first README-তে এই two-column comparison রাখতাম।

### Traditional

```c
int parse(const char *s)
{
    char *end;
    long v = strtol(s, &end, 10);

    if (errno == ERANGE)
        return -1;

    if (end == s)
        return -1;

    return (int)v;
}
```

### Nur

```c
nur_result_int nur_parse_int(const char *s)
{
    ...
    return NUR_OK(nur_result_int, value);
}
```

তারপর:

```c
if (NUR_IS_ERR(result))
    ...
```

---

# 189. Error context example

Final diagnostic:

```text
Configuration loading failed
  context: server.port

Caused by:
  invalid integer

code:
  NUR_E_PARSE
```

পরবর্তীতে:

```text
system:
  ERANGE
```

যোগ করা যায়।

---

# 190. Developer experience target

Developer যেন 10 মিনিটের মধ্যে বুঝে:

```text
How to define Result
How to return OK
How to return ERR
How to check
How to propagate
How to inspect error
```

এই five-step learning path অত্যন্ত গুরুত্বপূর্ণ।

---

# 191. Documentation design

README:

```text
1. Why Nur
2. Basic Result
3. Error codes
4. TRY
5. Option
6. Status
7. errno
8. C23
9. Ownership
10. Performance
11. Limitations
12. Examples
```

---

# 192. Documentation-এর সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ অংশ: “What Nur does NOT guarantee”

লিখবেন:

```text
Nur does not provide:
- memory safety
- automatic cleanup
- exception handling
- compiler-enforced exhaustive checking
- automatic ownership
```

এই honesty library-কে trustworthy করবে।

---

# 193. Design principle: no hidden side effects

Result construction ideally:

```text
does not log
does not allocate
does not mutate global state
does not throw
```

এই predictability valuable।

---

# 194. Structured error vs message

আমার preferred schema:

```text
              Error
               |
       +-------+-------+
       |       |       |
      code   native  message
       |      code      |
       |                |
 semantic           human text
```

Context/source optional metadata।

---

# 195. Potential error severity — not v1

Future:

```text
INFO
WARNING
ERROR
FATAL
```

কিন্তু সাধারণ Result-এর প্রতিটি failure automatically severity `ERROR` হবে—এটি ধরে নেওয়ার দরকার নেই।

আর severity application concern-ও হতে পারে।

---

# 196. Recoverability — also not v1

আপনি metadata দিতে পারেন:

```text
retryable
recoverable
```

কিন্তু automatic policy করবেন না।

কারণ:

```text
timeout
```

এক context-এ retryable, অন্য context-এ fatal হতে পারে।

---

# 197. Error code + capability

Future APIs:

```text
NUR_E_TIMEOUT
```

এর সঙ্গে application নিজে decide করবে:

```text
retry?
```

এতে library policy-free থাকে।

---

# 198. Result and logging

Application:

```c
if (NUR_IS_ERR(r)) {
    log_error(NUR_ERROR(r));
}
```

Nur itself:

```text
return error
```

এটাই cleaner separation।

---

# 199. Result and APIs

Nur functions should visibly identify fallibility.

Examples:

```c
nur_result_file nur_file_open(...);
nur_result_json nur_json_parse(...);
nur_result_int nur_parse_int(...);
nur_status_t nur_file_close(...);
```

এগুলি signature-level documentation।

---

# 200. This can change API design culture

Traditional:

```c
int foo();
```

Could mean:

```text
value
status
file descriptor
error code
```

Nur:

```c
nur_result_int foo();
```

communicates:

> This operation may fail.

এটি একটি real cultural improvement।

---

# 201. “Safe error handling” phrase refine করুন

আমি public docs-এ:

> **Explicit Error Handling**

ব্যবহার করব।

“Safe” শব্দটি secondary।

কারণ “safe” শুনে memory-safety guarantee মনে হতে পারে।

---

# 202. Best naming:

```text
NurResult — explicit result type for C
NurError — structured error information
NurOption — explicit optional value
NurStatus — result without payload
NurTry — explicit error propagation
```

এটি very coherent branding।

---

# 203. Potential Nur API ecosystem

```text
nur.h
├── Result
├── Error
├── Option
├── Status
├── Try
├── Panic
├── Assert
├── Diagnostics
├── POSIX adapters
└── Win32 adapters
```

এটাই long-term architecture।

---

# 204. Package variants

Potential:

```text
nur-core
nur-posix
nur-win32
nur-tools
nur-analyzer
```

একটি monorepo-তে।

---

# 205. Feature flags

Potential:

```c
#define NUR_ENABLE_SOURCE_LOCATION 1
#define NUR_ENABLE_ERROR_STRINGS 1
#define NUR_ENABLE_ASSERTS 1
#define NUR_ENABLE_C23 1
#define NUR_ENABLE_POSIX 0
```

কিন্তু flags বেশি হয়ে গেলে configuration nightmare হবে।

তাই first release-এ defaults carefully chosen রাখুন।

---

# 206. Better approach: compile profiles

```text
NUR_PROFILE_MINIMAL
NUR_PROFILE_STANDARD
NUR_PROFILE_DIAGNOSTIC
```

এটি individual flags-এর চেয়ে developer-friendly।

---

# 207. Minimal

```text
Result
Status
Option
Error code
```

---

# 208. Standard

```text
+ messages
+ context
+ errno adapter
+ Try
```

---

# 209. Diagnostic

```text
+ source location
+ formatting
+ assertions
+ panic
```

---

# 210. C23 profile

```text
+ [[nodiscard]]
+ typeof
+ C23 conveniences
```

---

# 211. Compiler extensions profile

GCC/Clang:

```text
statement expressions
__auto_type
attributes
```

কিন্তু clearly labeled:

```text
NUR_EXTENSIONS
```

---

# 212. Do not make extensions the default

Default should be:

```text
portable
predictable
standard C
```

Extensions:

```text
opt-in
```

---

# 213. Why this matters for Nur's reputation

A library that says:

> “Works with C”

but secretly depends on GCC is going to lose trust.

A library that says:

> C11 core; C23 enhanced; optional compiler extensions

is credible।

---

# 214. Current standard positioning

As of 2026, the published C standard is ISO/IEC 9899:2024. ([ISO][14])

So project documentation should say:

```text
Minimum supported:
C11

Recommended:
C23

Optional:
compiler extensions
```

---

# 215. Could minimum be C17 instead?

Possibly, but C11 gives you:

```text
_Generic
_Static_assert
_Thread_local
```

and other useful features. `_Generic` is particularly relevant to generic helpers. ([C++ Reference][13])

I would choose:

> **C11 core**

unless there is a strong product reason otherwise।

---

# 216. Why not C99?

You lose standard `_Generic`, and generic convenience becomes harder.

C11 is a sweet spot।

---

# 217. Why not C23 only?

Because you immediately shrink legacy C portability।

Better:

```text
C11 baseline
C23 enhancement
```

---

# 218. Static analyzer integration is potentially the real differentiator

Imagine a Nur project contains:

```text
nur.h
nur-analyzer
```

and analyzer understands:

```text
NUR_IS_OK(r)
NUR_IS_ERR(r)
NUR_VALUE(r)
NUR_ERROR(r)
```

তাহলে C code-এর static analysis stronger হতে পারে।

---

# 219. Analyzer rules

Possible warnings:

```text
NUR001 result discarded
NUR002 value read without success check
NUR003 error read in success path
NUR004 impossible Result state
NUR005 suspicious error-message lifetime
NUR006 ignored conversion
```

---

# 220. This is more ambitious than Result itself

At this point Nur becomes:

```text
library
+
convention
+
static analysis
```

এবং এটি actually could be differentiated from old `result.h` projects।

---

# 221. Compiler plugin না হলেও শুরু করা যায়

A standalone checker could parse:

```text
clang AST
```

and understand Nur macros/types।

Long term clang tooling is a sensible route।

---

# 222. IDE diagnostics

Example:

```text
NurResult value accessed without checking `is_ok`.
```

Code action:

```text
[Generate check]
```

Potential:

```c
if (NUR_IS_OK(result)) {
    ...
}
```

এটি developer experience enormously improve করবে।

---

# 223. Error handling code generation

Future tool:

```bash
nur new-result MyStruct
```

generate করবে:

```c
NUR_DEFINE_RESULT(MyStruct, nur_result_my_struct);
```

But this is tooling, not core.

---

# 224. Benchmark dashboard

Project website:

```text
NurResult vs status code

Success:
- ...
Failure:
- ...
Binary size:
- ...
```

তবে benchmark reproducibility instructions must be included।

---

# 225. Performance claims discipline

Allowed:

> “Benchmarks show X under GCC -O2 on Y hardware.”

Bad:

> “Zero-cost Result.”

Unless rigorously proven across representative cases।

---

# 226. API ergonomics metrics

You can measure:

```text
lines of code
branch count
number of manual error checks
number of errno accesses
number of cleanup branches
```

Example real-world benchmark:

```text
Traditional:
47 lines

Nur:
31 lines
```

এটি compelling।

---

# 227. But fewer lines is not automatically safer

One giant macro can make code shorter and harder to debug।

So benchmark:

```text
LOC
+
maintainability
+
diagnostic clarity
```

সব একসঙ্গে দেখুন।

---

# 228. Debuggability benchmark

A deliberate failure:

```text
invalid config
```

Compare diagnostics:

### C

```text
failed
```

### Nur

```text
NUR_E_PARSE
config.c:84
field="port"
system_code=...
message="invalid integer"
```

এটাই qualitative advantage।

---

# 229. Error observability

Future optional hooks:

```text
nur_error_observe(err)
```

for:

```text
metrics
telemetry
logging
```

Core should not impose them।

---

# 230. Integration with Sentry etc.

Optional application-layer adapters:

```text
NurError
   ↓
Sentry event
```

But never make third-party monitoring dependency part of core.

---

# 231. Serialization

Structured error can serialize into JSON:

```json
{
  "domain": "nur",
  "code": "NUR_E_NOT_FOUND",
  "message": "configuration file not found",
  "context": "startup"
}
```

This becomes useful for server software।

---

# 232. But core should not depend on JSON

Exactly the same principle:

```text
core = representation
adapter = serialization
```

---

# 233. Error localization

Machine code stays stable:

```text
NUR_E_NOT_FOUND
```

Message layer can become:

```text
English
Bangla
Arabic
etc.
```

No API logic breaks।

---

# 234. This makes Nur more future-proof than string-only Result

String-only Result:

```text
error message = API contract accidentally
```

Structured Result:

```text
error code = contract
message = presentation
```

Excellent distinction।

---

# 235. Error source classification

Potential:

```text
NUR_SOURCE_APPLICATION
NUR_SOURCE_SYSTEM
NUR_SOURCE_LIBRARY
NUR_SOURCE_USER
```

Not needed in v1।

---

# 236. Error cause hierarchy

Potential:

```text
source
  ↓
system
  ↓
adapter
  ↓
domain
  ↓
application
```

Example:

```text
EACCES
 ↓
NUR_E_PERMISSION
 ↓
ConfigError
 ↓
StartupError
```

This is long-term design direction।

---

# 237. API contract philosophy

Every function should answer three questions:

```text
What is success?
What is failure?
Who owns returned resources?
```

Nur docs should force this thinking।

---

# 238. Ownership should be documented separately

Create:

```text
docs/ownership.md
```

with terms:

```text
borrowed
owned
transferred
static
nullable
```

This can significantly reduce misuse।

---

# 239. Error ownership rules

For:

```c
const char *message;
```

say:

```text
borrowed/static
not freed
must remain valid
```

For:

```c
void *context;
```

I would avoid generic context pointers in v1 because ownership becomes unclear.

---

# 240. Use typed context instead

Instead of:

```c
void *context;
```

prefer:

```c
const char *context;
```

in core।

Domain-specific error structures can carry richer data।

---

# 241. Don't put `void *user_data` everywhere

Genericity through `void *` discards type information—the exact problem current WG14 proposals around typesafe parametric polymorphism are trying to address. ([Open Standards][16])

Nur should not repeat that mistake in its core abstractions।

---

# 242. Interesting future connection to C2y

WG14's 2026 “void-which-binds” proposal explicitly targets typesafe parametric polymorphism without runtime/ABI burden. ([Open Standards][16])

That suggests a fascinating future direction:

```text
Today:
macro-generated Result types

Future C:
stronger generic facilities
        ↓
simpler Nur implementation
```

So Nur architecture should avoid assumptions that block future standard generics।

---

# 243. Another future C direction: `_Generic`

WG14 has also been working on C2y improvements around `_Generic` expression evaluation/type safety. ([Open Standards][15])

Again:

> build Nur so future `_Generic` improvements can replace internal hacks, not force a redesign.

---

# 244. `NUR_TRY` and future C language evolution

Today:

```c
NUR_TRY_ASSIGN(...)
```

Future C may provide syntax that makes this cleaner.

Nur should isolate propagation internals behind a small macro API।

তাহলে internal implementation পরিবর্তন হলেও public source style stable থাকতে পারে।

---

# 245. Public API stability

Avoid exposing:

```text
internal union member names
private helper macros
compiler-specific internals
```

Use:

```c
NUR_VALUE
NUR_ERROR
NUR_IS_OK
```

as abstraction boundary।

---

# 246. Versioning

Use:

```c
#define NUR_VERSION_MAJOR 1
#define NUR_VERSION_MINOR 0
#define NUR_VERSION_PATCH 0
#define NUR_VERSION_STRING "1.0.0"
```

Semantic versioning।

---

# 247. Result type compatibility

Future v1 → v2 changes should preserve:

```text
OK semantics
ERR semantics
error code stability
macro meaning
```

Otherwise users cannot safely upgrade।

---

# 248. Error names should be stable API

This:

```text
NUR_E_NOT_FOUND
```

shouldn't rename to:

```text
NUR_E_RESOURCE_MISSING
```

every release।

---

# 249. But messages can evolve

```text
"file not found"
```

might become:

```text
"requested configuration file was not found"
```

without breaking application logic।

---

# 250. Documentation should distinguish API stability

```text
Stable:
    error codes

Semi-stable:
    diagnostic text

Debug-only:
    source file/line
```

Excellent practice।

---

# 251. Testing API itself

Potential test helpers:

```c
NUR_ASSERT_OK(r);
NUR_ASSERT_ERR(r);
NUR_ASSERT_ERROR_CODE(r, NUR_E_PARSE);
```

But these should perhaps be in:

```text
nur_test.h
```

not core։

---

# 252. Why test helper is valuable

Unit tests:

```c
TEST(parse_bad_port)
{
    auto r = parse_port("abc");

    NUR_ASSERT_ERR(r);
    NUR_ASSERT_ERROR_CODE(r, NUR_E_PARSE);
}
```

C doesn't have native `auto`, of course—this is pseudocode. Real API should stay C-valid.

---

# 253. Real C test style

```c
nur_result_int r = nur_parse_int("abc");

assert(NUR_IS_ERR(r));
assert(NUR_ERROR(r).code == NUR_E_PARSE);
```

Simple and portable।

---

# 254. Fuzz corpus

Repo:

```text
tests/fuzz/
```

include malformed:

```text
empty
huge
unicode
truncated
overflow
nested
invalid
```

especially parser functions।

---

# 255. Property testing

Potential invariant:

```text
if result.is_ok
    then error must not be consulted

if result.is_err
    then value must not be consulted
```

This can be tested indirectly via API discipline।

---

# 256. Documentation test

CI can compile every code example।

Command concept:

```text
docs examples
    ↓
extract
    ↓
compile
    ↓
run
```

This is valuable for macro-heavy library।

---

# 257. Compiler test grid

```text
GCC C11
GCC C17
GCC C23

Clang C11
Clang C17
Clang C23

MSVC C11-compatible mode
MSVC modern C support
```

Exact standard-mode support should be verified in CI rather than promised abstractly।

---

# 258. C23 `nodiscard` test

```c
NUR_NODISCARD
nur_result_int dangerous_operation(void);
```

Then:

```c
dangerous_operation();
```

should produce diagnostic where supported।

C23 `nodiscard` explicitly allows a rationale string too, which could be interesting for Nur. ([C++ Reference][11])

---

# 259. Great idea: reason in the nodiscard message

C23 supports:

```c
[[nodiscard("handle the NurResult")]]
```

Conceptually:

```text
warning:
ignoring result: handle the NurResult
```

This is developer-friendly.

---

# 260. C11 GCC equivalent

Potential:

```c
__attribute__((warn_unused_result))
```

GCC documents this specifically for functions whose result must be used. ([GCC][12])

---

# 261. `NUR_NODISCARD` macro should abstract it

```c
#if ...
    #define NUR_NODISCARD [[nodiscard]]
#elif ...
    #define NUR_NODISCARD __attribute__((warn_unused_result))
#else
    #define NUR_NODISCARD
#endif
```

Implementation details should be private।

---

# 262. Could `NUR_DEFINE_RESULT` itself be nodiscard?

C23 allows `nodiscard` to apply to a struct/union declaration, and a function returning such a type by value can then trigger diagnostics on discard. ([C++ Reference][11])

That means a C23 design can potentially place nodiscard semantics directly on generated Result types.

This is one of the most elegant modern-C connections for Nur.

---

# 263. Proposed C23 mode

Conceptually:

```c
typedef struct [[nodiscard]] {
    ...
} nur_result_int;
```

The exact generated placement and portability should be tested across supported C23 compilers, but the language facility exists. ([C++ Reference][11])

---

# 264. Result access safety with analyzer

Imagine:

```c
NUR_VALUE(r)
```

macro expands to:

```c
(r).data.value
```

Analyzer can identify that pattern.

So don't hide Result implementation in a million opaque macros.

Keep semantic macros simple enough for tooling.

---

# 265. This is very important

Good macro library:

```text
easy for compiler
easy for analyzer
easy for debugger
```

Bad macro library:

```text
preprocessor maze
```

---

# 266. Debugger integration

Could create pretty-printer scripts eventually:

```text
GDB
LLDB
```

so Result is shown as:

```text
OK(42)
```

instead of:

```text
{is_ok=1, data={value=42}}
```

That is a fantastic developer-experience feature.

---

# 267. GDB pretty-printer future

Example:

```text
(gdb) print result
NurResult<int>::Ok(42)
```

or:

```text
Err(NUR_E_TIMEOUT)
```

This is not core, but very useful.

---

# 268. IDE debugger visualization

VS Code debugger could show:

```text
state: ERR
code: NUR_E_PERMISSION
message: "..."
```

rather than raw struct fields।

---

# 269. Documentation site

Potential sections:

```text
Getting Started
API Reference
Design Philosophy
Error Model
Ownership
C11
C23
POSIX
Windows
Performance
Examples
FAQ
Migration Guide
```

---

# 270. Migration guide is important

Old:

```c
int fd = open(...);
if (fd < 0) ...
```

New:

```c
nur_result_fd fd = nur_open(...);
if (NUR_IS_ERR(fd)) ...
```

Old:

```c
char *p = malloc(...);
if (!p) ...
```

New:

```c
nur_result_ptr p = nur_malloc(...);
```

This gives immediate value to existing C developers।

---

# 271. Gradual migration

Do not require rewriting entire application.

Offer:

```text
nur_wrap_*
```

or adapters:

```text
nur_fopen
nur_strtol
nur_malloc
nur_open
```

Then teams can migrate subsystem-by-subsystem।

---

# 272. Wrapper philosophy

Old API:

```text
returns sentinel
```

Nur wrapper:

```text
captures
normalizes
returns Result
```

This is probably the best adoption strategy։

---

# 273. Standard library adapter candidates

Potential:

```text
strtol
strtoul
malloc/calloc/realloc
fopen
fread
fwrite
fclose
remove
rename
time operations
```

Only where a Result abstraction meaningfully improves semantics।

---

# 274. Don't wrap everything

Avoid:

```text
strlen
memcpy
abs
```

with Result unless there is actual fallibility։

This is crucial for library ergonomics।

---

# 275. “Fallible API” as a design category

A function is **fallible** when its operation can legitimately fail in ordinary runtime circumstances.

Examples:

```text
I/O
allocation
parsing
network
resource acquisition
validation
```

Result is appropriate।

---

# 276. Non-fallible API

Examples:

```text
simple arithmetic
pure accessor
constant lookup
```

Result may only add noise։

---

# 277. API naming can communicate fallibility

Potential convention:

```text
nur_parse_int()
nur_open_file()
```

are clearly fallible by Result return type।

No need for:

```text
nur_try_parse_int()
```

because Result already communicates it।

---

# 278. This avoids another redundancy

Don't have:

```text
nur_parse_int
nur_try_parse_int
nur_safe_parse_int
nur_checked_parse_int
```

Result is the contract।

---

# 279. Error contract in API docs

Example:

```text
nur_parse_int

Success:
    returns parsed integer

Possible errors:
    NUR_E_INVALID_ARGUMENT
    NUR_E_PARSE
    NUR_E_OUT_OF_RANGE
```

Excellent developer experience।

---

# 280. Error enum should be intentionally small

Core:

```text
NUR_E_INVALID_ARGUMENT
NUR_E_NOT_FOUND
NUR_E_PERMISSION
NUR_E_IO
NUR_E_PARSE
NUR_E_TIMEOUT
NUR_E_CANCELLED
NUR_E_OUT_OF_MEMORY
NUR_E_UNSUPPORTED
NUR_E_INTERNAL
```

Enough।

---

# 281. User-defined errors

Could support:

```c
#define NUR_E_USER_BASE 1000
```

but numeric ranges alone become awkward।

Better long-term:

```text
domain + code
```

---

# 282. Domain model

```c
typedef struct {
    int domain;
    int code;
    ...
} nur_error_t;
```

Then:

```text
domain = NUR_DOMAIN_CORE
code = NUR_E_PARSE
```

or:

```text
domain = MYAPP_DOMAIN_AUTH
code = APP_E_TOKEN_EXPIRED
```

This is scalable।

---

# 283. Do this in v2, not v1

Keep first release simple.

---

# 284. `Result<T,E>` vs fixed `nur_error_t`

A philosophical question:

Should Nur support:

```text
Result<T,E>
```

or only:

```text
Result<T,nur_error_t>
```

?

### Generic error E

More powerful.

### Fixed nur_error_t

Much easier propagation and ecosystem consistency.

My recommendation:

> **v1: fixed `nur_error_t`; v2+: optionally explore typed/custom error domains.**

---

# 285. Why fixed error is good

Then:

```text
Result<int>
Result<File>
Result<Config>
```

সব একই error model ব্যবহার করে।

`NUR_TRY_ASSIGN` simpler।

---

# 286. Typed error can return later

Future macro:

```c
NUR_DEFINE_RESULT_EX(Type, ErrorType, Name)
```

Possible।

But don't make it first-class before base API is stable।

---

# 287. Optional custom Result

Potential:

```c
NUR_DEFINE_RESULT_EX(
    MyType,
    MyError,
    my_result
);
```

Advanced users can opt in।

---

# 288. This creates two APIs

Basic:

```text
nur_result_int
```

Advanced:

```text
my_result
```

Good compromise।

---

# 289. Option genericity

Same question:

```text
NurOption<T>
```

Need macro-generated types।

C11 `_Generic` can help helpers but does not give you native parameterized types. ([C++ Reference][13])

---

# 290. `NurOption` example

```c
NUR_DEFINE_OPTION(int, nur_option_int);

nur_option_int find_value(...);
```

States:

```text
Some(42)
None
```

---

# 291. Option accessors

```text
NUR_OPTION_IS_SOME
NUR_OPTION_IS_NONE
NUR_OPTION_VALUE
```

and perhaps:

```text
NUR_OPTION_UNWRAP
NUR_OPTION_VALUE_OR
```

But keep core small।

---

# 292. Result API should not expose Option internals

`Result`:

```text
OK / ERR
```

Option:

```text
Some / None
```

Different domain models.

---

# 293. Status API

Possible:

```c
typedef struct {
    bool is_ok;
    nur_error_t error;
} nur_status_t;
```

Simple।

---

# 294. `NUR_STATUS_OK`

```c
#define NUR_STATUS_OK() \
    ((nur_status_t){ .is_ok = true })
```

and:

```c
NUR_STATUS_ERR(error)
```

---

# 295. This also improves APIs like `close`

```c
nur_status_t nur_file_close(file);
```

No meaningless:

```text
dummy void result
```

---

# 296. Resource ownership in Result

Potential file Result:

```c
nur_result_file
```

Success includes:

```text
handle
```

Documentation:

```text
Caller owns the returned handle and must close it.
```

This is crucial.

---

# 297. Could Result automatically close resources?

No.

Without destructors/RAII, automatic resource management requires scope tricks or ownership runtime.

Keep Result and resource ownership separate।

---

# 298. Future C “defer” helps but remains optional

Exactly।

---

# 299. NurResult versus `setjmp/longjmp`

```text
setjmp/longjmp
    = control-flow transfer

NurResult
    = explicit data flow
```

Nur is more composable and visible.

---

# 300. NurResult versus global error state

```text
global last error
    → implicit

Result
    → explicit
```

This is a core principle.

---

# 301. “Error travels with value” is a great phrase

Possible branding:

> **The error travels with the result.**

It communicates the architecture immediately।

---

# 302. Potential logo/branding aside

You could brand the architecture:

```text
NUR RESULT
```

with visual:

```text
✓ value
✕ error
```

But technical API remains clean।

---

# 303. Do not overbrand the code

Code should look like C:

```c
nur_result_int
NUR_OK
NUR_ERR
```

not:

```text
NUR_MAGIC_RESULT_EXEC(...)
```

---

# 304. Error logging format

Potential default:

```text
[NUR_E_PERMISSION] failed to open config
  context: config.toml
  source: config.c:84
  system: EACCES
```

Human-readable but machine-derived from structured fields।

---

# 305. JSON format

Optional:

```json
{
  "code": "NUR_E_PERMISSION",
  "system_code": 13,
  "message": "failed to open config",
  "context": "config.toml"
}
```

Again adapter, not core।

---

# 306. Telemetry format

Structured error means:

```text
error_code=NUR_E_TIMEOUT
operation=http_get
retry_count=2
```

can be measured without parsing strings।

---

# 307. Observability benefit

This makes Nur more useful in production C systems than simple Result wrappers।

---

# 308. Error metrics

Application can count:

```text
NUR_E_TIMEOUT
NUR_E_NOT_FOUND
NUR_E_OUT_OF_MEMORY
```

without text processing।

---

# 309. Alerting

Operations can alert:

```text
timeout rate > threshold
```

using stable error code.

---

# 310. This is a significant real-world justification

Not:

> Rust syntax in C.

But:

> **Structured failures that machines and humans can both understand.**

---

# 311. Error string consistency

Messages should ideally be:

```text
lowercase
specific
no unnecessary punctuation
```

Go's official style guidance also emphasizes concise error strings and contextual composition. ([Go][20])

Nur can adopt a similar style, though it doesn't need to copy Go exactly।

---

# 312. Example

Good:

```text
"invalid port"
```

Bad:

```text
"ERROR!!! SOMETHING WENT WRONG!!!"
```

Machine-readable code does the categorization; message gives human detail।

---

# 313. Contextual error composition

Better:

```text
failed to load config: permission denied
```

than:

```text
permission denied
```

But structured context means you need not jam everything into one string।

---

# 314. Recommended internal representation

My final v1 choice:

```c
typedef struct {
    nur_error_code_t code;
    int system_code;

    const char *message;
    const char *context;

    const char *file;
    const char *function;
    unsigned line;
} nur_error_t;
```

This is small enough to be practical and rich enough to be useful।

---

# 315. Result representation

```c
typedef enum {
    NUR_RESULT_ERR = 0,
    NUR_RESULT_OK  = 1
} nur_result_state_t;

#define NUR_DEFINE_RESULT(Type, Name) \
    typedef struct { \
        nur_result_state_t state; \
        union { \
            Type value; \
            nur_error_t error; \
        } data; \
    } Name;
```

---

# 316. Result invariant

Document this in bold:

```text
state == NUR_RESULT_OK
    → value is the meaningful member

state == NUR_RESULT_ERR
    → error is the meaningful member
```

This is the central invariant।

---

# 317. Public accessors

```c
NUR_IS_OK(r)
NUR_IS_ERR(r)
NUR_VALUE(r)
NUR_ERROR(r)
```

Keep them trivial۔

---

# 318. Don't make `NUR_VALUE()` perform unexpected logging

It should either:

```text
raw access
```

or:

```text
debug assertion
```

but never hidden logging।

---

# 319. Two access APIs could be useful

Unsafe:

```c
NUR_VALUE(r)
```

Checked:

```c
NUR_UNWRAP(r)
```

But naming should make risk obvious।

---

# 320. Potentially even better naming

```text
NUR_VALUE_UNCHECKED
```

would be safer semantically, but verbose।

I would keep:

```text
NUR_VALUE
```

and documentation says check state first.

---

# 321. Analyzer can enforce that convention

Future static tooling:

```text
NUR_VALUE(r)
```

outside checked branch → warning.

This is the long-term answer।

---

# 322. Result state could be encoded as enum

Better debugging:

```text
NUR_RESULT_OK
NUR_RESULT_ERR
```

rather than:

```text
true/false
```

Potentially more self-documenting।

---

# 323. But `bool` size might be smaller?

Not necessarily meaningful; compiler layout/alignment governs final struct. Don't optimize based on assumptions; measure।

---

# 324. Union size

If:

```text
T = 4 bytes
nur_error_t = 56 bytes
```

Result will be dominated by error representation.

This is one reason diagnostic structs must not become huge.

---

# 325. Source location cost

Three pointers plus an unsigned line can make `nur_error_t` significantly larger on 64-bit systems.

Therefore:

```text
minimal
```

and:

```text
diagnostic
```

profiles make sense.

---

# 326. A more minimal core error

Potential:

```c
typedef struct {
    nur_error_code_t code;
    int system_code;
    const char *message;
} nur_error_t;
```

Then diagnostic metadata stays in separate debug structure։

This is even more lightweight.

---

# 327. Which is better?

### Compact core

```text
code
system_code
message
```

Best for performance.

### Rich diagnostic

```text
+ context
+ file
+ function
+ line
```

Best for developer experience.

My recommendation:

> Keep source fields conditional/configurable.

---

# 328. Compile-time diagnostic switch

Concept:

```c
#if NUR_ENABLE_SOURCE_LOCATION
...
#endif
```

When disabled:

```text
smaller error object
```

---

# 329. This needs ABI caution

If public ABI depends on configuration:

```text
app compiled with one profile
library with another
```

layouts may diverge.

Header-only is easier.

---

# 330. Therefore if Nur is header-only:

configuration specialization is much less painful.

---

# 331. If later compiled library:

freeze a public ABI layout and put diagnostics into an optional separate object。

---

# 332. Error object vs diagnostic object

Advanced clean architecture:

```text
nur_error_t
    = semantic failure

nur_error_diag_t
    = presentation/debug metadata
```

Then Result holds:

```text
nur_error_t
```

and debug subsystem can associate diagnostics separately।

But complexity।

---

# 333. v1 should avoid this separation

Use one practical struct।

---

# 334. Why Result's error should never be just `errno`

Because:

```text
errno
```

is system-oriented.

Nur needs semantic abstraction:

```text
NUR_E_PERMISSION
```

while preserving:

```text
EACCES
```

Both are useful।

---

# 335. Semantic normalization

This is a core capability Nur can own.

---

# 336. `strerror()` should be late

Store:

```text
system_code
```

and format later.

Do not necessarily store:

```text
strerror(errno)
```

pointer as semantic error identity।

This avoids coupling Result lifetime to a mutable/static implementation-specific diagnostic buffer.

---

# 337. Error code names

```text
code name
```

is stable.

Message:

```text
human text
```

is not.

---

# 338. Error contexts should be short

Bad:

```text
5000-character object dump
```

Good:

```text
"server.port"
```

or:

```text
"/etc/app/config.toml"
```

when safe।

---

# 339. Sensitive context warnings

Documentation:

> Never store secrets or credentials in `nur_error_t.context` or `message`.

Excellent practice।

---

# 340. Result and concurrency

Result can be passed by value:

```c
nur_result_int r = foo();
```

This is naturally composable.

No mutex required around the Result object itself if ownership isn't shared.

---

# 341. But payload may be shared

For:

```c
nur_result_ptr
```

thread safety depends on pointed object.

Result does not magically make payload thread-safe.

---

# 342. “Result makes C safe” is therefore too broad

Correct:

> **Result makes failure state explicit.**

Excellent.

---

# 343. “Result prevents null dereference” is also too broad

Unless static analyzer or checked accessor catches misuse.

Raw C can still do:

```c
NUR_VALUE(err_result)
```

and misuse data।

---

# 344. So the safety stack is:

```text
Result
+
API discipline
+
nodiscard
+
debug assertions
+
static analysis
+
tests
```

The combination creates practical safety improvements।

---

# 345. This is the real architecture

```text
                 NUR.H
                   |
       +-----------+-----------+
       |                       |
   DATA MODEL             DISCIPLINE
       |                       |
   Result<T>               nodiscard
   Option<T>               TRY
   Status                  IGNORE
       |
    Error
       |
 code/system/message/context
       |
   ADAPTERS
   /       \
POSIX     Win32
       |
   DIAGNOSTICS
       |
 analyzer / debugger / telemetry
```

---

# 346. Feature ranking

এখন feature-এর ranking দিলে:

| Rank | Feature                    | Importance                     |
| ---: | -------------------------- | ------------------------------ |
| 🥇 1 | `NurResult<T>`             | Essential                      |
| 🥇 2 | `NUR_TRY_ASSIGN`           | Essential                      |
| 🥇 3 | `NUR_NODISCARD`            | Essential                      |
| 🥇 4 | `nur_error_t`              | Essential                      |
|    5 | `errno` adapter            | Very high                      |
|    6 | `NurStatus`                | High                           |
|    7 | `NurOption`                | High                           |
|    8 | source/context diagnostics | High                           |
|    9 | POSIX/Win32 mapping        | High                           |
|   10 | panic/assert               | Medium                         |
|   11 | error wrapping             | Medium                         |
|   12 | monadic APIs               | Medium                         |
|   13 | defer                      | Medium/future                  |
|   14 | static analyzer            | 🚀 Major future differentiator |
|   15 | debugger tooling           | 🚀 Major UX feature            |

---

# 347. If you only build two features

Build:

```text
NurResult
+
NUR_TRY
```

But…

# 348. If you want developer “wow”

Build:

```text
NurResult
+
NUR_TRY
+
NUR_NODISCARD
+
structured NurError
```

That is the strongest four-feature combination।

---

# 349. If you want ecosystem “wow”

Build:

```text
NurResult
NurError
NurOption
NurStatus
NurTry
C23 nodiscard
errno adapters
Win32 adapters
static analyzer
```

Now you're no longer just another Result header।

---

# 350. If you want truly ambitious “groundbreaking” positioning

Long-term:

```text
NurResult
+
NurError
+
NurOption
+
NurStatus
+
NurTry
+
source-aware diagnostics
+
OS normalization
+
static analyzer
+
IDE integration
+
debugger visualizers
+
benchmark suite
+
C11/C23 dual-mode
```

এটি একটি **tooling ecosystem**।

---

# 351. What should NOT be advertised

Don't advertise:

```text
❌ Rust safety in C
❌ memory-safe C
❌ bug-free C
❌ no crashes
❌ zero-cost guaranteed
❌ replaces errno everywhere
❌ first Result type in C
```

---

# 352. What SHOULD be advertised

```text
✅ Explicit error handling
✅ Typed operation outcomes
✅ Structured errors
✅ Compiler-assisted result discipline
✅ Rust-inspired Result semantics
✅ Allocation-free core option
✅ C11-compatible foundation
✅ C23 enhancements
✅ POSIX/Windows error normalization
✅ Clear error propagation
```

---

# 353. Best technical tagline

My favorite:

> **Typed outcomes. Explicit errors. Plain C.**

---

# 354. More descriptive tagline

> **A portable, C-native Result/Option error-handling toolkit with explicit error propagation and structured diagnostics.**

Excellent for GitHub README।

---

# 355. Best “why” paragraph

> C APIs often communicate failure through conventions that vary by function: sentinel return values, status codes, output parameters, and `errno`. Nur introduces a small, explicit vocabulary for fallible operations: a typed Result carries either a successful value or structured error information, while compiler-assisted `nodiscard` and ergonomic propagation helpers reduce accidental error handling omissions.

This accurately describes the problem without overstating C's limitations. POSIX explicitly documents both `errno`-based and direct error-number conventions, while C23 provides `[[nodiscard]]` for discarded results. ([The Open Group][9])

---

# 356. Why “Rust-inspired” is better than “Rust for C”

Rust:

```text
Result
ownership
borrowing
lifetimes
pattern matching
must_use
?
```

Nur:

```text
struct
union
macro
enum
nodiscard
static analysis
```

The latter is **not Rust's safety model**।

---

# 357. The most accurate technical relationship

```text
Rust
    ↓ inspiration

Go
    ↓ explicit error-return ergonomics

C23
    ↓ language facilities

POSIX/Win32
    ↓ system errors

Nur
    ↓ integrates all of them
```

This is an excellent conceptual identity।

---

# 358. Why C23 matters particularly

C23 is now the published C standard, ISO/IEC 9899:2024. ([ISO][14])

Its `[[nodiscard]]` feature maps almost perfectly to Result-returning functions: the standard explicitly allows the compiler to diagnose discarded struct/union/enum results when the type/function is marked appropriately. ([C++ Reference][11])

That means Nur can build modern ergonomics **without inventing a proprietary compiler**।

---

# 359. Why `_Generic` matters

C11 `_Generic` provides compile-time type selection and is already used to create portable type-generic interfaces. ([C++ Reference][13])

Nur can leverage that for:

```text
typed helpers
dispatch
printing
conversion
validation
```

without abandoning standard C11.

---

# 360. Why future C proposals matter

WG14's ongoing work around `_Generic` and stronger type/generic facilities shows that the language is still evolving in exactly the areas that could eventually make libraries like Nur easier to implement. ([Open Standards][15])

Therefore:

> **Design Nur so its abstraction layer survives improvements in the language.**

---

# 363. Nur 3.x

The ambitious layer:

```text
nur-analyzer
```

এই পর্যায়ে library ecosystem-এর real differentiation শুরু হবে।

---

# 364. Exact v1 philosophy

আমি v1-এর জন্য এই 8টি rule লিখতাম:

```text
1. Failure must be explicit.
2. Errors must be machine-readable.
3. Messages must be human-readable.
4. Core must not allocate.
5. Core must not log implicitly.
6. Core must not depend on an OS.
7. C11 must remain supported.
8. C23 may enhance but must not be required.
```

এই philosophy খুব শক্ত।

---

# 365. Exact v1 feature set

```text
[CORE]

NurResult<T>
NurError
NurStatus

[CONSTRUCTION]

NUR_OK
NUR_ERR
NUR_MAKE_ERROR

[INSPECTION]

NUR_IS_OK
NUR_IS_ERR
NUR_VALUE
NUR_ERROR

[PROPAGATION]

NUR_TRY_ASSIGN
NUR_IGNORE

[PORTABILITY]

C11

[MODERN]

C23 NODISCARD
```

---

# 366. Exact v1 non-feature set

```text
NO
    exceptions
    hidden globals
    mandatory malloc
    automatic cleanup
    automatic retries
    logging
    JSON
    async runtime
    huge macro framework
```

---

# 367. This keeps the project credible

A first release that tries to solve:

```text
error
memory
async
logging
network
serialization
```

all at once becomes difficult to understand.

A first release that solves:

> **fallible function outcomes**

is coherent।

---

# 368. A possible “Nur Result law”

You could define:

> **A fallible Nur API returns either an expected value or a structured error; it never uses an undocumented sentinel to represent failure.**

This is an excellent project convention।

---

# 369. Another law

> **Every public fallible Result-returning function should be `NUR_NODISCARD`.**

Except functions whose discard semantics are explicitly documented।

---

# 370. Another law

> **Errors are values, not side effects.**

This is probably the best architectural statement।

---

# 371. Another

> **Semantic errors are identified by codes, never by message text.**

Very strong।

---

# 372. Another

> **System errors are preserved, not exposed as the application's primary error vocabulary.**

Excellent distinction।

---

# 373. Another

> **Nur does not own what it does not allocate.**

This is a good ownership principle for messages/context.

---

# 374. Another

> **Recoverable failure is returned; violated invariants are asserted.**

This separates error and bug paths।

---

# 375. Potential FAQ

### “Does Nur replace errno?”

Answer:

> Not at the system boundary. Nur captures and normalizes system errors into structured application-level errors.

This is more accurate than “yes।”

---

# 376. “Is Nur memory safe?”

Answer:

> No. Nur improves explicit error handling; it does not provide Rust-like ownership or memory safety.

Necessary honesty।

---

# 377. “Is Nur zero-cost?”

Answer:

> The core representation is deliberately lightweight and can be allocation-free, but actual cost depends on payload size, ABI, diagnostics, optimization, and compiler.

Very defensible।

---

# 378. “Why not just use integers?”

Answer:

> Integers can encode failure, but they do not inherently carry both a typed success value and structured failure information.

Good।

---

# 379. “Why not just use errno?”

Answer:

> `errno` is useful at system/API boundaries, but it is not a uniform application-level result type. Nur preserves system error codes while adding explicit semantic structure. POSIX itself documents APIs that return errors directly rather than through `errno`. ([The Open Group][2])

---

# 380. “Why not just use Go?”

Answer:

> Nur is for projects that must remain in C—for ABI, embedded environments, existing codebases, OS interfaces, performance or deployment constraints—but want more explicit failure semantics.

---

# 381. “Why not Rust?”

Same idea:

> Rust is the stronger language-level solution when changing languages is viable; Nur targets situations where the project remains C.

This is strategically important।

---

# 382. This also prevents bad marketing

Nur should not pretend:

```text
C + Nur = Rust
```

Instead:

```text
C + Nur = better C error conventions
```

Much more believable।

---

# 383. Competitive positioning

Your actual competitors aren't only Result libraries.

They're:

```text
1. traditional C style
2. status-code APIs
3. errno patterns
4. bespoke project-specific error structs
5. setjmp/longjmp exception frameworks
6. C++ expected
7. Rust Result
8. Go-style explicit errors
9. existing C Result macros
```

Nur's job is to occupy:

```text
standard-ish
C-native
portable
structured
minimal
developer-friendly
```

---

# 384. The most defensible uniqueness claim

Not:

> “First.”

Instead:

> **Nur aims to provide a cohesive, portable Result/Option error-handling vocabulary for modern C, combining explicit typed outcomes, structured system-aware errors, ergonomic propagation, and compiler-assisted diagnostics.**

That is a serious proposition।

---

# 385. If Nur becomes mature, developers could write

```c
nur_result_config load_config(const char *path)
{
    nur_result_text text;
    NUR_TRY_ASSIGN(
        nur_result_text,
        text,
        nur_read_file(path)
    );

    nur_result_config config;
    NUR_TRY_ASSIGN(
        nur_result_config,
        config,
        nur_parse_config(NUR_VALUE(text))
    );

    return config;
}
```

And caller:

```c
nur_result_config r = load_config("app.toml");

if (NUR_IS_OK(r)) {
    run_app(NUR_VALUE(r));
} else {
    report_error(NUR_ERROR(r));
}
```

এটি readability-এর দিক থেকে অত্যন্ত শক্তিশালী।

---

# 386. Final architecture I would choose

```text
                         ┌─────────────────┐
                         │     nur.h       │
                         └────────┬────────┘
                                  │
                 ┌────────────────┼────────────────┐
                 │                │                │
             NurResult        NurStatus       NurOption
                 │
             NurError
                 │
       ┌─────────┼─────────┐
       │         │         │
      code    system    diagnostic
       │        code         │
       │                   ┌─┴──────────────┐
       │                   │                │
    semantic            message          context
                            │
                    source location
                 │
        ┌────────┴─────────┐
        │                  │
       POSIX             Win32
        │                  │
      errno          GetLastError
                 │
              NurTry
                 │
           [[nodiscard]]
                 │
       ┌─────────┴─────────┐
       │                   │
     CI/tests          static analyzer
                           │
                    IDE/debugger tools
```

# 387. My final ranking of what would make `nur.h` genuinely special

### Level S — absolutely build

```text
NurResult
NurError
NUR_TRY_ASSIGN
NUR_NODISCARD
```

### Level A — high-value

```text
NurOption
NurStatus
errno normalization
source/context diagnostics
allocation-free core
C11/C23 support
```

### Level B — mature library

```text
error wrapping
error chain
formatting
panic/assert
POSIX/Win32 adapters
```

### Level C — ecosystem

```text
map
and_then
map_error
defer
resource helpers
```

### Level S+ — potentially the real differentiator

```text
Nur static analyzer
IDE integration
GDB/LLDB visualizers
automatic error-contract documentation
compiler-assisted Result-state analysis
```

# 388. The single most important insight

আপনার original idea-এর সবচেয়ে শক্তিশালী version হলো:

```text
                DON'T JUST BUILD
                 "Result in C"

                       ↓

                 BUILD A SYSTEM

        Result
          +
        Error
          +
        Status
          +
        Option
          +
        Try
          +
      NODISCARD
          +
     errno/Win32
          +
     diagnostics
          +
     analyzer
```

এখানেই `nur.h` একটি সাধারণ GitHub snippet থেকে একটি **serious developer library**-তে পরিণত হতে পারে।

---

# 389. “The Best Feature” যদি একটিই বাছতে হয়

**`NurResult + NUR_TRY + NUR_NODISCARD`-কে একটি single flagship capability হিসেবে ধরুন।**

কারণ শুধু:

```c
struct + union
```

developer-কে অবাক করবে না।

কিন্তু:

```c
nur_result_config load_config(...);
```

তারপর:

```c
NUR_TRY_ASSIGN(...)
```

এবং ভুলে গেলে:

```text
compiler warning
```

—এই পুরো experience-টাই meaningful।

C23-এর `[[nodiscard]]` এবং GCC-এর `warn_unused_result` precisely এমন compiler-assisted discipline-এর building blocks দেয়। ([C++ Reference][11])

---

# 390. আমার strongest proposed definition of NurResult

> **NurResult is a C-native tagged outcome abstraction that explicitly represents either successful data or structured failure, with optional compiler-assisted diagnostics and ergonomic error propagation.**

বাংলায়:

> **NurResult হলো C-এর জন্য একটি native tagged outcome abstraction, যা একটি operation-এর সফল value অথবা structured error-কে একই typed result-এর মধ্যে explicitভাবে প্রকাশ করে এবং `nodiscard` ও propagation helper-এর মাধ্যমে ভুলভাবে error ignore করার সম্ভাবনা কমায়।**

এটি আপনার original ধারণার সবচেয়ে technically mature form।

---

# 391. আর `nur.h`-এর সবচেয়ে ambitious vision

```text
                      NUR
                       │
           ┌───────────┴───────────┐
           │                       │
       LANGUAGE                  TOOLING
           │                       │
      Result/Option             Analyzer
      Error/Status              IDE
      Try                       Debugger
      Nodiscard                 Docs
           │                       │
      ┌────┴────┐             ┌────┴────┐
      │         │             │         │
     C11       C23          Static    Runtime
      │         │            checks   diagnostics
      └────┬────┘
           │
      POSIX / Win32
           │
       Applications
```

এই vision-এ `nur.h` শুধু একটি header নয়; এটি **modern error-handling conventions for C**-এর foundation হতে পারে।

সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ বিষয় হলো, এটি বাস্তব C-এর constraints মেনে করতে হবে—Rust-এর safety দাবি করে নয়, বরং **C-এর নিজের primitives দিয়ে explicitness, consistency এবং tooling উন্নত করে**। বর্তমান C standard হিসেবে ISO/IEC 9899:2024 এবং C23-এর `[[nodiscard]]`/C11 `_Generic`—দুটিই এই strategy-র জন্য relevant foundations। ([ISO][14])
